# specrt model coverage

Fetched 2026-11-24. Generated by `crates/specrt/scripts/model_coverage.sh`; do not edit by hand.

## Main labs

Source: `tests/fixtures/popular/labs.toml`. Per lab: every model its hub orgs released since 2026-02-24 (9 months) plus every family the lab still ships, one row per repo of the lab itself with its release date. Quantised copies by other orgs sit under the source lab and are counted, not listed. A model over one card is never refused for size: it reads SUPPORTED-WITH-OFFLOAD, or `needs family #N, then offload: ...` when the architecture is also missing. Machine read for the offload numbers: 28 GB free host RAM (75% usable by the host tier), 602 GB free NVMe, 2 cards of 96 GB.

Image pipelines (diffusers `model_index.json`), classified by the family file that matches the pipeline class (`family-flux2_klein.toml`, #1285). Size is the sum of the text encoder, denoiser and VAE safetensors; one image job runs per card and the text encoder is released after encoding. The FP8 and NVFP4 klein copies and the 9B are not listed until the denoiser reads those weights.

## Would load if quantised

Rows over one card whose bf16 weights the quantise at load path can shrink: the same placement planner reruns with the weights scaled by the quantised size of the whole model (nvfp4_g16 and fp8_channel sizes from `anatomy::quant_bytes`, the family keep list left in bf16). Only rows that change class are listed: to LOADS (one card), or from DOES-NOT-FIT to SUPPORTED-WITH-OFFLOAD. fp8_channel does not cover mixture-of-experts. ## Parked gaps

Listed in `gaps.toml` under `parked`: gaps decided out of scope for the server, kept out of the ranked tables. Reopen through the issue.

| gap | blocked downloads | models | issue | reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| model_type llada | 0.11 M | GSAI-ML/LLaDA-1.5, mradermacher/LLaDA-1.5-GGUF, GSAI-ML/LLaDA-8B-Instruct, FunAGI/LLaDA-8B-Instruct-gptqmodel-4bit, mradermacher/LLaDA-8B-Instruct-i1-GGUF | #1336 | masked diffusion LM: every step re-runs the whole block bidirectionally, so no KV reuse and a separate scheduler loop; not on the main-lab list and vLLM does not serve it. The forward pass is the llama family with a non-causal mask |

"Popular" means popular on Hugging Face on 2026-11-24: the most downloaded and the trending repos for the text-generation, image-text-to-text and any-to-any pipeline tags, as the hub API ranked them that day. Downloads are the hub's last-30-day counts. Nothing here was measured by loading weights: every class comes from the model's `config.json` (and the hub's file list for GGUF repos) run through specrt's own checks, `Spec::from_hf_config_serving`, `Vision::from_hf_config` and `Gguf::spec`, with the family specs embedded in this build.

Source: Hugging Face API: pipeline_tag text-generation (sort downloads, limit 1000; sort trendingScore, limit 200), image-text-to-text (downloads 400, trending 100) and any-to-any (downloads 100); downloads are the hub's last-30-day counts.

Selection: every model of the labs in labs.toml released since 2026-02-24 or still shipped by its lab, then top 100 text groups by summed downloads, the top 20 trending text groups not already in, the top 30 vision groups not already in, and every text model in the local hub cache; a group is every repo whose base_model chain ends at the same root; repos whose names look like test fixtures are skipped. Each group keeps the original lab's repo (`base`) and the most downloaded NVFP4, FP8, AWQ, GPTQ and GGUF copy (`nvfp4`, `fp8`, `awq`, `gptq`, `gguf`), plus any copy already in the local hub cache (`disk`).

## Classes

- LOADS: the serving check passes and every input tower the config declares is read.
- LOADS-TEXT-ONLY: the text model passes; a vision or audio tower in the config is refused or not read, so only text input works.
- SUPPORTED-WITH-OFFLOAD: the architecture passes and the weights are more than one 96 GB card holds after its 2.1 GB reserve. This is not a refusal: placement spreads layers over the cards, then host RAM, then NVMe. The why column gives the cards, host RAM and NVMe the tiers need on this machine (read from the manifest's `machine` block) and whether `PlacementConfig::place` accepts it today with the desktop's device list.
- DOES-NOT-FIT: the architecture passes but the weights exceed every tier together on this machine; the why column says by how much.
- UNKNOWN-QUANT: the architecture passes without the quantisation, the quantisation format is refused.
- REFUSED: the architecture itself is missing or refused; the why column is the exact refusal text. A refused model that is also over one card reads `needs family #N, then offload: ...` in the Main labs tables. Size never refuses a model.
- UNCHECKED: nothing specrt could check was found: gated with no open mirror, or a GGUF repo whose hub metadata names only the vision projector.

## Ranked gaps

The same gaps as above, ordered by the summed downloads of the rows they block. This is the older view and it is not the queue order: the lab priority at the top of this section is, because downloads reward CI and tutorial traffic. A base row counts its whole group less the quantised copies listed beside it. Chat and tokenizer gaps are counted only on rows whose architecture passes. The issue column maps a gap to its tracker issue through `crates/specrt/tests/fixtures/popular/gaps.toml`; every gap over 1.00 M downloads has one.

## Quantisation formats in the safetensors indexes

Modules per weight layout, counted from each repo's `model.safetensors.index.json` (tensor names only). Read today means at least one repo holding that layout classifies as LOADS, LOADS-TEXT-ONLY or SUPPORTED-WITH-OFFLOAD.

| layout | modules | repos | read today |
|---|---|---|---|
| compressed-tensors int4 (weight_packed+weight_scale) | 2247665 | 75 | no |
| compressed-tensors nvfp4 (weight_packed+weight_global_scale) | 681091 | 40 | yes |
| fp8 block (weight_scale_inv) | 2510558 | 80 | yes |
| fp8 or int8 per-channel (weight_scale) | 1533257 | 104 | yes |
| gptq/awq qweight+qzeros | 1547525 | 88 | no |
| modelopt nvfp4 (weight_scale_2) | 2824993 | 74 | yes |
| mxfp4 (_blocks/_scales) | 120 | 2 | yes |
| plain weight | 2523394 | 554 | yes |

GGUF tensor types specrt reads: BF16, F16, F32, IQ1_M, IQ1_S, IQ2_S, IQ2_XS, IQ2_XXS, IQ3_S, IQ3_XXS, IQ4_NL, IQ4_XS, Q4_0, Q4_K, Q5_0, Q5_K, Q6_K, Q8_0. A GGUF file label counts as read when its main type is; K-quant mixes also hold other types, which only a real load checks.

## Needs a real load

A config check cannot prove these; they stay unproven until a real load (phase 2):

- Every LOADS row without a tiny fixture in its family: the config passes, but no logits check covers the family.
- GGUF rows: classified from the hub's architecture field and file names, never from the file header (no range requests). A K-quant mix may hold a type specrt does not read.
- Rows whose weights the hub sizes above one card: the planner's offload and multi-card paths decide those.
